- **bigquery**: Use for any BigQuery request — running SQL over GTM data, reading INFORMATION_SCHEMA, creating views, materialized views or scheduled queries, or working with the GA4 or Google Ads exports. BigQuery is the easiest platform to run queries against and the hardest to get wrong operationally, because its failure mode is not a 429, it is an invoice. Dry-run every generated query and set maximumBytesBilled on every job. BigQuery has no native metric or semantic layer: if a customer's metrics live only here, they live in SQL text and any metric the agent produces is reinvention.
